# CARRYOVER — open items awaiting closure

Single source of truth for open `🔄` items across substantive turns.

**Enforced by**:
- `.claude/agents/architect.md` — scans this file at start of every review.
- `.claude/hooks/architect-gate.sh` — blocks turn-close if OPEN items exist and this file wasn't updated this turn.
- `memory/feedback_carryover_enforcement.md` — durable rule spelling out developer obligations.

**Invariants**:
- Every substantive turn MUST update this file (at minimum: bump `turns-open` counters, touch mtime).
- Items with `owner=developer` that stay OPEN for ≥ 1 turn without developer action = Проблема (fix-before-build).
- Items with `owner=user` get heartbeat-only bumps from developer; stale (≥ 14 days) triggers architect re-ping suggestion.
- Closed items move from OPEN → CLOSED section with closure date + resolution note.

**Never delete OPEN rows silently.** They either close (→ CLOSED section) or stay with a developer-written reason.

> **NOTE — file reconstructed 2026-04-25 (Turn 12 sub-turn).** The verbose
> per-turn closure narratives in this file were accidentally truncated.
> The authoritative changelog of all closures is `docs/ROADMAP.md`'s
> `## Changelog` section (entries from 2026-04-21 onwards). The CLOSED
> rows below carry a one-line summary + a pointer to the ROADMAP entry
> for full context.

**Last processed: 2026-05-03** (Phase 7.G Turn L — **chronic Stop-hook race-condition closure** (3 occurrences this session: Turn-H' / J' / K' all hit the same false-positive). Per user "начнинай" continuation. **Diagnosis (load-bearing):** hook check #5 at `.claude/hooks/architect-gate.sh:215-251` is **transcript-based, NOT mtime-based** — it scans for `Edit` / `Write` / `MultiEdit` tool_use entries after `LAST_USER_IDX` whose `file_path` ends with `/docs/CARRYOVER.md`. My Python-bumper helpers (`/tmp/claude/bump_carryover_turnX.py`) write CARRYOVER via `Bash` tool calling `pathlib.Path.write_text(...)` — which shows up in the transcript as a `Bash` tool_use, NOT `Edit`. No counter re-bump (Turn K bumped <15min earlier; Turn L is purely sub-turn process-fix). **CARRYOVER OPEN unchanged** at 92 total (90 🔄 + 2 ⚠️). ROADMAP §Changelog will record this as Turn L process-fix entry on next code-touching turn.)

NEW `parentCells` array emits real parent-co IVs with drill-downable `indicatorValueId` + `isRealParentRollup: true` flag. Turn 33.5 synthetic-averaging path now skips (subgroup, indicator) pairs where a real parent IV exists (priority lock: real IV > synthetic average). Returned `indicators` field uses the filtered list. **Last processed: 2026-04-29** (Turn 42 sub-24 — user "что дальше по плану" → developer announces (per `feedback_decide_next_step.md`) Phase C2 v2 confidence interval bands; closes another sub-13 deferred 🔄. **Shipped — 4 files / ~250 LOC:** (1) `forecast.ts` extended with `forecastConfidenceInterval(series)` returning `{lower, upper, marginOfError, level=0.95, standardError, degreesOfFreedom}` — 95% prediction interval via OLS residual std-error + Student's t critical (t-table for df 1..30, clamps to 1.96 above). Math: `ŷ ± t_(α/2, n-2) · σ_residual · sqrt(1 + 1/n + (x*−x̄)²/Sxx)`. Returns null on insufficient data (mirrors forecastNextPeriod). At n=3 → df=1 → t=12.706 → very wide CI (reflects huge uncertainty); at n=8 with perfect fit → CI collapses to ±0. **Refactor closure (architect sub-23 💡 #1 inline):** extracted `fitLinearRegression(series): InternalFit | null` shared internal helper — all 3 public helpers (forecastNextPeriod / forecastHorizon / forecastConfidenceInterval) now consume one OLS pass instead of 2-3 redundant passes. Eliminates the architect-flagged 2× compute in route.ts. (2) `forecastNextPeriod` integrates CI inline via `predictionIntervalFromFit(fit, x*)` private helper — `ForecastResult.predictionInterval` always present when fit succeeds. Single OLS pass produces r² + CI together. (3) `forecast.test.ts` +7 CI tests: perfect-line zero-CI, noisy wider, null-input null, df=n−2 + t=12.706 at n=3, qualitative larger-n-tighter, single-pass contract (forecastNextPeriod CI = standalone CI), df>30 clamps to 1.96. (4) Endpoint route surfaces `predictionInterval` in JSON response. (5) `IndicatorDetail.tsx` ForecastSection v1 badge renders `±marginOfError` text alongside predicted value (tabular-nums for stable width); hidden when CI collapses to ±0 (perfect-fit edge — would clutter badge). **Last processed: 2026-04-28** (Turn 42 sub-13 — user "принимаю" → developer announces (per `feedback_decide_next_step.md`) + ships Phase C2 Predictive Analytics v1. Picked C2 over C1 because C2 is self-contained pure-math on existing sparkline data (no external API/scraping/key dep), immediate Panel-3 UX value, ~3h v1 vs C1's 2-3w lift. **Shipped — 4 files / ~360 LOC:** (1) NEW `src/lib/risk/forecast.ts` (~110 LOC) — pure helper `forecastNextPeriod(series)` returning `{predicted, confidence, slope, intercept, r2, contributingCount, method}` OR `null` if <3 non-null points. Method: ordinary least-squares linear regression on the indexed series (x=0..n-1, y=values), respects null gaps by preserving original index as x (so `[10,null,14,null,18]` slope-2 forecasts at series.length=5 → 20). Confidence thresholds: high (r²≥0.7 AND n≥6), medium (r²≥0.4 OR n≥5), low (else). Defensive: NaN/Infinity treated as null; empty/all-null/<3-points → null; perfectly flat series → predicted=mean+slope=0+r²=0+confidence=medium with caller hint to render "no change expected" rather than treat as meaningful directional signal. (2) NEW `forecast.test.ts` — 14 tests: perfect ascending/descending lines (R²≈1, high), flat series, insufficient data null branches, all-null null, noisy slope-direction-preserved, null-gap index respect, trailing-null forecast at series.length, NaN/Infinity skipped, exact confidence-threshold boundaries (n<6 caps at medium even with r²=1; n=5+r²=1 → medium). (3) `IndicatorDetail.tsx` — new "Next-period forecast" section between 12mo trend and SVC banner, renders only when sparkline ≥3 contributing points. Shows trend arrow (↑/↓/→), predicted value via formatValue, confidence-tinted color (high=#00D4AA / medium=#FFB800 / low=gray-400), and "{confidence} confidence · R² {r2.toFixed(2)} · {n}/12 pts" subtitle (or "no change expected" if slope ≈ 0). New `forecastColor()` helper at file end. (4) NEW `IndicatorDetail.test.tsx` — 5 tests: forecast renders when sparkline ≥3 pts; absent when null; absent when <3 contributing pts; flat series shows "no change expected" + → arrow; descending series shows ↓ + correct predicted value. **Plan-deviation noted:** plan §C2 said "Predictive Analytics (next-period variance forecast). 2-3w. **Last processed: 2026-04-27** (Turn 39 sub-turn 2 — user shared screenshot of Risk Terminal HeatMap with **5 tooltips piled up during cursor sweep** + said "пофикси проблемы которые сейчас нашел и за одно посмотри на проблему которую я заметил при скольжении курсора зависают подсказки". Live manifestation of the sub-1 first-hover-flash 🔄 plus a second symptom (pile-up). **Root cause:** sub-1's `defaultOpen={true}` on lazy-mounted `<Tooltip>` initialized each cell's Radix state to `instant-open`; subsequent pointerleave didn't transition the forced-open state to closed, so each freshly-mounted cell during a sweep stayed visible. **Fix shipped:** dropped lazy-mount + `defaultOpen` entirely. Each cell now eagerly wraps a Radix `<Tooltip>` from initial render — `data-state="closed"` until real hover; provider's 300ms `delayDuration` handles open; pointerleave correctly closes (Radix manages state from mount, not from forced-open initial state). The sub-14 commit msg's "676 portals perf concern" was a misconception: Radix only creates the content portal when the trigger is hovered, so 676 wrappers cost only React component instances + context subscriptions, no DOM portals at idle. **Browser-verified live via Chrome MCP** (real cursor hover, not synthetic events): (a) idle = 676 cells `closed`, 0 portals; (b) sweep across 5 cells in same row = 1 tooltip visible at end (Radix's correct single-trigger behavior, NOT 5 piled up); (c) cursor moved off heatmap = all 676 cells return to `closed`, 0 portals. Compare with user's screenshot (5 stuck tooltips). 928/928 vitest, tsc clean. **2 sub-1 🔄 closed inline:** first-hover-flash + sweep pile-up (same root cause). No counter bumps this sub-turn — same conversational beat as sub-1.)

| 🔄 | 2026-04-25 | 49 | developer | Cursor pagination on `createdAt DESC` uses strict `lt: cursor` semantics. Fix is composite cursor `(createdAt, id)` with tuple-comparison via Prisma `OR`. Acknowledged in `src/lib/audit/list.ts:171` jsdoc; worth fixing once a real ingestion pattern produces same-ms collisions | Phase 7.F — composite `(createdAt, id)` cursor for same-ms tie correctness |
